Real Drydock logs omit `result.stats` for\nsome calls, write `cost_usd: null` for providers that report no cost, and could carry a field of an\nunexpected JSON type. None of these may drop a record or fail the run.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- `result.stats.input_tokens`, `cached_input_tokens`, `output_tokens`, `cost_usd`, and `elapsed_ms`\n  are each read independently.\n- A field that is absent or JSON `null` counts as zero.\n- A field present with a JSON type other than number or `null` (string, boolean, array, object)\n  counts as zero and the record is kept (decision Q-004, `zero-keep`).\n- A record with no `result.stats` object at all is still a record; every numeric field is zero.\n- `result.returncode` that is JSON `null` or absent is recorded as absent, not as zero.\n- `result.timed_out` that is absent or not a boolean is recorded as `false`.\n- Numbers are decoded with `encoding/json`; integer fields tolerate values written like `12.0`\n  only if they are whole numbers, otherwise they count as zero.\n- Nothing here writes to the log being read.\n\n## Required Go Tests\n\nThe build writes these tests in package `./internal/logread`, each asserting the behavior its name\nstates against a log or line written by the test itself:\n\n- `TestAbsentStatsIsStillRecord`\n- `TestNullCostCountsZero`\n- `TestAbsentNumbersCountZero`\n- `TestWrongTypedNumbersCountZero`\n- `TestReturncodeNullOrAbsentIsAbsent`\n- `TestTimedOutDefaultsFalse`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC absent-numbers-no-stats ===\nIntent: A record with no result.stats object is kept as a record with zero numeric fields.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/logread\", \"TestAbsentStatsIsStillRecord\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC absent-numbers-no-stats ===\n\n=== AC absent-numbers-null-cost ===\nIntent: A null cost_usd counts as zero while the other numeric fields of the record are still summed.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/logread\", \"TestNullCostCountsZero\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC absent-numbers-null-cost ===\n\n=== AC absent-numbers-absent-fields ===\nIntent: Each absent token count, cost, and duration counts as zero independently of the others.\nRequires: executable=go; Reports group by what ran, and fall back\nto the request only when the log does not say.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- A record's model is `result.stats.model` when it is present and a non-empty string.\n- Otherwise the model is `job.model`.\n- A `result.stats` object without `model`, a missing `result.stats`, a `null` model, or an empty\n  string model all fall back to `job.model`.\n- The provider of a record is `job.llm`.\n- The model and provider are exposed on the record for the models and failures reports.\n\n## Required Go Tests\n\nThe build writes these tests in package `./internal/logread`, each using lines written by the test:\n\n- `TestModelPrefersExecuted`\n- `TestModelFallsBackWhenStatsAbsent`\n- `TestModelFallsBackWhenEmpty`\n- `TestProviderIsJobLLM`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC model-attribution-executed ===\nIntent: A record with a non-empty result.stats.model is attributed to that model rather than job.model.\nRequires: executable=go; no model name is hardcoded.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-DateOptions.md ===\n# FEATURE: Date Options\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261026 V1 |\n| Description | The --since and --until option values are accepted only as real calendar dates written YYYY-MM-DD. |\n| Depends On  | FEATURE-CommandLine.md |\n| Provides    | cli date validation |\n| Consumes    | ddkstat <report> [--target] [--since] [--until] <log> |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nDate filters compare against the first 10 characters of `started_at`, so an option value must be\nexactly that shape and must name a day that exists.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- A valid value is exactly ten characters: four-digit year, `-`, two-digit month, `-`, two-digit day.\n- The value must also be a real calendar date, checked with `time.Parse` and the `2006-01-02`\n  layout; leap days are honored (`02-29` is valid only in a leap year).\n- Rejected: month 13, day 30 in February, non-padded forms such as `2026-1-5`, compact forms such\n  as `20261014`, trailing or leading characters, an empty value, and any non-date text.\n- The validator is a pure function in `internal/cli` returning the date text and an error; the\n  parser maps its error to a usage error (exit handling is owned by FEATURE-UsageErrors.md).\n\n## Required Go Tests\n\nThe build writes these tests in package `./internal/cli`:\n\n- `TestParseDateAcceptsRealDates`\n- `TestParseDateRejectsMalformed`\n- `TestParseDateRejectsImpossibleDates`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC date-options-accepts-real ===\nIntent: Real YYYY-MM-DD dates, including a leap day, are accepted by the date validator.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/cli\", \"TestParseDateAcceptsRealDates\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC date-options-accepts-real ===\n\n=== AC date-options-rejects-malformed ===\nIntent: Non-padded, compact, padded, empty, and non-date values are rejected by the date validator.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/cli\", \"TestParseDateRejectsMalformed\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC date-options-rejects-malformed ===\n\n=== AC date-options-rejects-impossible ===\nIntent: Well-formed but impossible dates such as month 13 or 30 February are rejected.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/cli\", \"TestParseDateRejectsImpossibleDates\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC date-options-rejects-impossible ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- Only the standard library is used for date validation.\n- A date is never silently normalized; an invalid value is rejected, not corrected.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-UsageErrors.md ===\n# FEATURE: Usage Errors\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261026 V1 |\n| Description | Every usage error exits 2 with nothing on standard output and a usage line on standard error. |\n| Depends On  | FEATURE-DateOptions.md |\n| Provides    | exit code 2 |\n| Consumes    | cli date validation |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nOperators and scripts distinguish a mistyped command (exit 2) from an unreadable log (exit 1).\n\n## Behavior\n\n- Exit 2, with empty standard output, for: no arguments; an unknown report; an unknown option\n  (including `--help`, decision Q-001 `unknown-exit-2`); an option missing its value; an invalid\n  date value; a missing log path; more than one log path.\n- Usage errors are decided before the log is opened, so a usage error never becomes exit 1 even if\n  the named log does not exist.\n- One usage line is written to standard error naming each of the six reports (`summary`,\n  `commands`, `targets`, `models`, `daily`, `failures`) and each of the three options (`--target`,\n  `--since`, `--until`). Standard error is never compared by acceptance.\n- A repeated option is not a usage error: the last occurrence wins (decision Q-002).\n\n## Required Go Tests\n\nThe build writes this test in package `./internal/cli`, writing the usage line to a `bytes.Buffer`\nand checking it names every report and every option:\n\n- `TestUsageLineNamesReportsAndOptions`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC usage-errors-exit-2 ===\nIntent: Each kind of usage error exits 2 and writes nothing to standard output, with an existing log path supplied.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nimport json\nimport os\nimport tempfile\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\nfd, log = tempfile.mkstemp(suffix=\".jsonl\")\nwith os.fdopen(fd, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n    f.write(json.dumps({\"execution_id\": \"a\", \"started_at\": \"2026-10-14T00:00:00.000Z\",\n                        \"status\": \"succeeded\",\n                        \"job\": {\"command_name\": \"build\", \"target\": \"T\"}}) + \"\\n\")\n\ncases = {\n    \"no-args\": [],\n    \"unknown-report\": [\"frobnicate\", log],\n    \"missing-log\": [\"summary\"],\n    \"unknown-option\": [\"summary\", \"--colour\", \"red\", log],\n    \"help-option\": [\"summary\", \"--help\", log],\n    \"extra-log\": [\"summary\", log, log],\n    \"missing-value-target\": [\"summary\", \"--target\"],\n    \"missing-value-since\": [\"summary\", \"--since\"],\n    \"missing-value-until\": [\"summary\", \"--until\"],\n    \"bad-since-month\": [\"summary\", \"--since\", \"2026-13-01\", log],\n    \"bad-until-day\": [\"summary\", \"--until\", \"2026-02-30\", log],\n    \"bad-since-form\": [\"summary\", \"--since\", \"2026-1-5\", log],\n}\ntry:\n    for name, args in cases.items():\n        code, out, err = cli_runner(*args)\n        print(name, code)\n        print(err)\n        assert code == 2, (name, code, out, err)\n        assert out == \"\", (name, out)\nfinally:\n    os.unlink(log)\n=== END AC usage-errors-exit-2 ===\n\n=== AC usage-errors-before-open ===\nIntent: A usage error takes precedence over an unreadable log, so it exits 2 rather than 1.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nimport os\nimport tempfile\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\nmissing = os.path.join(tempfile.gettempdir(), \"ddkstat-no-such-log-usage.jsonl\")\nassert not os.path.exists(missing)\n\nfor args in ([\"frobnicate\", missing],\n             [\"summary\", \"--since\", \"2026-13-01\", missing],\n             [\"summary\", \"--colour\", \"red\", missing]):\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(*args)\n    print(args, code)\n    print(err)\n    assert code == 2, (args, code, out, err)\n    assert out == \"\", (args, out)\n=== END AC usage-errors-before-open ===\n\n=== AC usage-errors-usage-line ===\nIntent: The usage line names every report and every option.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/cli\", \"TestUsageLineNamesReportsAndOptions\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC usage-errors-usage-line ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- A usage error never writes to standard output.\n- Diagnostics go to standard error only and are never part of the report contract.\n- `--help` is an ordinary unknown option; no help mode exists.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-UnreadableLog.md ===\n# FEATURE: Unreadable Log\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261026 V1 |\n| Description | A log that cannot be opened or read exits 1 with nothing on standard output. |\n| Depends On  | FEATURE-LogReading.md, FEATURE-CommandLine.md |\n| Provides    | exit code 1 |\n| Consumes    | logread.Read, ddkstat <report> [--target] [--since] [--until] <log> |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nAn operational failure reading the log is reported differently from a usage error so that callers\ncan tell a bad invocation from a bad environment.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- A log path that does not exist, cannot be opened, or cannot be read exits 1.\n- A directory named as the log path is unreadable and exits 1 (decision Q-003, `exit-1`).\n- One diagnostic line is written to standard error; standard output stays empty.\n- Report output is buffered until the whole log has been read, so a read error after some lines\n  were consumed cannot leave partial standard output.\n- This applies to every report name.\n- The log is only ever opened for reading.\n\n## Required Go Tests\n\nThe build writes this test in package `./internal/cli`, using a reader that fails partway through:\n\n- `TestReadErrorLeavesStdoutEmpty`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC unreadable-log-missing-file ===\nIntent: A missing log exits 1 with empty standard output for every report.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nimport os\nimport tempfile\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\nmissing = os.path.join(tempfile.gettempdir(), \"ddkstat-no-such-log-unreadable.jsonl\")\nassert not os.path.exists(missing)\n\nfor report in (\"summary\", \"commands\", \"targets\", \"models\", \"daily\", \"failures\"):\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(report, missing)\n    print(report, code)\n    print(err)\n    assert code == 1, (report, code, out, err)\n    assert out == \"\", (report, out)\n=== END AC unreadable-log-missing-file ===\n\n=== AC unreadable-log-directory ===\nIntent: A directory named as the log exits 1 with empty standard output.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nimport os\nimport tempfile\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\ndirectory = tempfile.mkdtemp()\ntry:\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(\"summary\", directory)\n    print(code)\n    print(err)\n    assert code == 1, (code, out, err)\n    assert out == \"\", out\nfinally:\n    os.rmdir(directory)\n=== END AC unreadable-log-directory ===\n\n=== AC unreadable-log-no-partial-output ===\nIntent: A read error partway through the log leaves standard output empty.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/cli\", \"TestReadErrorLeavesStdoutEmpty\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC unreadable-log-no-partial-output ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- The log is never opened for writing and is never modified.\n- An unreadable log never writes to standard output.\n- An unreadable log exits 1, never 0 or 2.\n=== END ARTIFACT ==="}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":82800,"cache_read_input_tokens":1568,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":82800},"output_tokens":7,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"global","speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"dc9d2e6a-5c44-44ca-adf3-edd22471c077","uuid":"25b1dbe4-490b-4e2d-9268-de1196f5259d","timestamp":"2026-10-26T01:25:07.261Z","request_id":"req_011CgPyW4NQq9aoGAXYGTzsH"}

BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-AbsentNumbers.md ===\n# FEATURE: Absent Numbers\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261026 V1 |\n| Description | Absent, null, or wrongly typed token counts, cost, and durations count as zero without dropping the record. |\n| Depends On  | FEATURE-DuplicateIds.md |\n| Provides    | logread.Record numeric fields |\n| Consumes    | logread.Read |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nThe reporter sums token counts, cost, and elapsed time. Real Drydock logs omit `result.stats` for\nsome calls, write `cost_usd: null` for providers that report no cost, and could carry a field of an\nunexpected JSON type. None of these may drop a record or fail the run.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- `result.stats.input_tokens`, `cached_input_tokens`, `output_tokens`, `cost_usd`, and `elapsed_ms`\n  are each read independently.\n- A field that is absent or JSON `null` counts as zero.\n- A field present with a JSON type other than number or `null` (string, boolean, array, object)\n  counts as zero and the record is kept (decision Q-004, `zero-keep`).\n- A record with no `result.stats` object at all is still a record; every numeric field is zero.\n- `result.returncode` that is JSON `null` or absent is recorded as absent, not as zero.\n- `result.timed_out` that is absent or not a boolean is recorded as `false`.\n- Numbers are decoded with `encoding/json`; integer fields tolerate values written like `12.0`\n  only if they are whole numbers, otherwise they count as zero.\n- Nothing here writes to the log being read.\n\n## Required Go Tests\n\nThe build writes these tests in package `./internal/logread`, each asserting the behavior its name\nstates against a log or line written by the test itself:\n\n- `TestAbsentStatsIsStillRecord`\n- `TestNullCostCountsZero`\n- `TestAbsentNumbersCountZero`\n- `TestWrongTypedNumbersCountZero`\n- `TestReturncodeNullOrAbsentIsAbsent`\n- `TestTimedOutDefaultsFalse`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC absent-numbers-no-stats ===\nIntent: A record with no result.stats object is kept as a record with zero numeric fields.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/logread\", \"TestAbsentStatsIsStillRecord\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC absent-numbers-no-stats ===\n\n=== AC absent-numbers-null-cost ===\nIntent: A null cost_usd counts as zero while the other numeric fields of the record are still summed.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/logread\", \"TestNullCostCountsZero\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC absent-numbers-null-cost ===\n\n=== AC absent-numbers-absent-fields ===\nIntent: Each absent token count, cost, and duration counts as zero independently of the others.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/logread\", \"TestAbsentNumbersCountZero\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC absent-numbers-absent-fields ===\n\n=== AC absent-numbers-wrong-type ===\nIntent: A numeric field with a JSON type other than number or null counts as zero and keeps the record.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/logread\", \"TestWrongTypedNumbersCountZero\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC absent-numbers-wrong-type ===\n\n=== AC absent-numbers-returncode ===\nIntent: A null or absent returncode is recorded as absent rather than zero.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/logread\", \"TestReturncodeNullOrAbsentIsAbsent\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC absent-numbers-returncode ===\n\n=== AC absent-numbers-timed-out ===\nIntent: An absent timed_out defaults to false.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/logread\", \"TestTimedOutDefaultsFalse\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC absent-numbers-timed-out ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- A record is never dropped because a numeric field is missing, null, or of the wrong type.\n- Never write to the log being read.\n- No value is hardcoded; every number comes from the log being read.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-ModelAttribution.md ===\n# FEATURE: Model Attribution\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261026 V1 |\n| Description | Each record is attributed to the executed model, falling back to the requested model, under its provider. |\n| Depends On  | FEATURE-AbsentNumbers.md |\n| Provides    | logread.Record.Model |\n| Consumes    | logread.Read |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`job.model` is the requested model alias (for example `sonnet`), while `result.stats.model` is the\nmodel that actually ran (for example `claude-sonnet-5-5`). Reports group by what ran, and fall back\nto the request only when the log does not say.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- A record's model is `result.stats.model` when it is present and a non-empty string.\n- Otherwise the model is `job.model`.\n- A `result.stats` object without `model`, a missing `result.stats`, a `null` model, or an empty\n  string model all fall back to `job.model`.\n- The provider of a record is `job.llm`.\n- The model and provider are exposed on the record for the models and failures reports.\n\n## Required Go Tests\n\nThe build writes these tests in package `./internal/logread`, each using lines written by the test:\n\n- `TestModelPrefersExecuted`\n- `TestModelFallsBackWhenStatsAbsent`\n- `TestModelFallsBackWhenEmpty`\n- `TestProviderIsJobLLM`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC model-attribution-executed ===\nIntent: A record with a non-empty result.stats.model is attributed to that model rather than job.model.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/logread\", \"TestModelPrefersExecuted\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC model-attribution-executed ===\n\n=== AC model-attribution-no-stats ===\nIntent: A record without result.stats is attributed to job.model.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/logread\", \"TestModelFallsBackWhenStatsAbsent\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC model-attribution-no-stats ===\n\n=== AC model-attribution-empty ===\nIntent: A null, absent, or empty result.stats.model falls back to job.model.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/logread\", \"TestModelFallsBackWhenEmpty\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC model-attribution-empty ===\n\n=== AC model-attribution-provider ===\nIntent: The provider of a record is read from job.llm.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/logread\", \"TestProviderIsJobLLM\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC model-attribution-provider ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- The executed model is never replaced by the requested model when the executed model is present and non-empty.\n- Attribution reads only the log record; no model name is hardcoded.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-DateOptions.md ===\n# FEATURE: Date Options\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261026 V1 |\n| Description | The --since and --until option values are accepted only as real calendar dates written YYYY-MM-DD. |\n| Depends On  | FEATURE-CommandLine.md |\n| Provides    | cli date validation |\n| Consumes    | ddkstat <report> [--target] [--since] [--until] <log> |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nDate filters compare against the first 10 characters of `started_at`, so an option value must be\nexactly that shape and must name a day that exists.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- A valid value is exactly ten characters: four-digit year, `-`, two-digit month, `-`, two-digit day.\n- The value must also be a real calendar date, checked with `time.Parse` and the `2006-01-02`\n  layout; leap days are honored (`02-29` is valid only in a leap year).\n- Rejected: month 13, day 30 in February, non-padded forms such as `2026-1-5`, compact forms such\n  as `20261014`, trailing or leading characters, an empty value, and any non-date text.\n- The validator is a pure function in `internal/cli` returning the date text and an error; the\n  parser maps its error to a usage error (exit handling is owned by FEATURE-UsageErrors.md).\n\n## Required Go Tests\n\nThe build writes these tests in package `./internal/cli`:\n\n- `TestParseDateAcceptsRealDates`\n- `TestParseDateRejectsMalformed`\n- `TestParseDateRejectsImpossibleDates`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC date-options-accepts-real ===\nIntent: Real YYYY-MM-DD dates, including a leap day, are accepted by the date validator.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/cli\", \"TestParseDateAcceptsRealDates\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC date-options-accepts-real ===\n\n=== AC date-options-rejects-malformed ===\nIntent: Non-padded, compact, padded, empty, and non-date values are rejected by the date validator.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/cli\", \"TestParseDateRejectsMalformed\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC date-options-rejects-malformed ===\n\n=== AC date-options-rejects-impossible ===\nIntent: Well-formed but impossible dates such as month 13 or 30 February are rejected.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/cli\", \"TestParseDateRejectsImpossibleDates\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC date-options-rejects-impossible ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- Only the standard library is used for date validation.\n- A date is never silently normalized; an invalid value is rejected, not corrected.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-UsageErrors.md ===\n# FEATURE: Usage Errors\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261026 V1 |\n| Description | Every usage error exits 2 with nothing on standard output and a usage line on standard error. |\n| Depends On  | FEATURE-DateOptions.md |\n| Provides    | exit code 2 |\n| Consumes    | cli date validation |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nOperators and scripts distinguish a mistyped command (exit 2) from an unreadable log (exit 1).\n\n## Behavior\n\n- Exit 2, with empty standard output, for: no arguments; an unknown report; an unknown option\n  (including `--help`, decision Q-001 `unknown-exit-2`); an option missing its value; an invalid\n  date value; a missing log path; more than one log path.\n- Usage errors are decided before the log is opened, so a usage error never becomes exit 1 even if\n  the named log does not exist.\n- One usage line is written to standard error naming each of the six reports (`summary`,\n  `commands`, `targets`, `models`, `daily`, `failures`) and each of the three options (`--target`,\n  `--since`, `--until`). Standard error is never compared by acceptance.\n- A repeated option is not a usage error: the last occurrence wins (decision Q-002).\n\n## Required Go Tests\n\nThe build writes this test in package `./internal/cli`, writing the usage line to a `bytes.Buffer`\nand checking it names every report and every option:\n\n- `TestUsageLineNamesReportsAndOptions`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC usage-errors-exit-2 ===\nIntent: Each kind of usage error exits 2 and writes nothing to standard output, with an existing log path supplied.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nimport json\nimport os\nimport tempfile\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\nfd, log = tempfile.mkstemp(suffix=\".jsonl\")\nwith os.fdopen(fd, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n    f.write(json.dumps({\"execution_id\": \"a\", \"started_at\": \"2026-10-14T00:00:00.000Z\",\n                        \"status\": \"succeeded\",\n                        \"job\": {\"command_name\": \"build\", \"target\": \"T\"}}) + \"\\n\")\n\ncases = {\n    \"no-args\": [],\n    \"unknown-report\": [\"frobnicate\", log],\n    \"missing-log\": [\"summary\"],\n    \"unknown-option\": [\"summary\", \"--colour\", \"red\", log],\n    \"help-option\": [\"summary\", \"--help\", log],\n    \"extra-log\": [\"summary\", log, log],\n    \"missing-value-target\": [\"summary\", \"--target\"],\n    \"missing-value-since\": [\"summary\", \"--since\"],\n    \"missing-value-until\": [\"summary\", \"--until\"],\n    \"bad-since-month\": [\"summary\", \"--since\", \"2026-13-01\", log],\n    \"bad-until-day\": [\"summary\", \"--until\", \"2026-02-30\", log],\n    \"bad-since-form\": [\"summary\", \"--since\", \"2026-1-5\", log],\n}\ntry:\n    for name, args in cases.items():\n        code, out, err = cli_runner(*args)\n        print(name, code)\n        print(err)\n        assert code == 2, (name, code, out, err)\n        assert out == \"\", (name, out)\nfinally:\n    os.unlink(log)\n=== END AC usage-errors-exit-2 ===\n\n=== AC usage-errors-before-open ===\nIntent: A usage error takes precedence over an unreadable log, so it exits 2 rather than 1.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nimport os\nimport tempfile\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\nmissing = os.path.join(tempfile.gettempdir(), \"ddkstat-no-such-log-usage.jsonl\")\nassert not os.path.exists(missing)\n\nfor args in ([\"frobnicate\", missing],\n             [\"summary\", \"--since\", \"2026-13-01\", missing],\n             [\"summary\", \"--colour\", \"red\", missing]):\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(*args)\n    print(args, code)\n    print(err)\n    assert code == 2, (args, code, out, err)\n    assert out == \"\", (args, out)\n=== END AC usage-errors-before-open ===\n\n=== AC usage-errors-usage-line ===\nIntent: The usage line names every report and every option.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/cli\", \"TestUsageLineNamesReportsAndOptions\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC usage-errors-usage-line ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- A usage error never writes to standard output.\n- Diagnostics go to standard error only and are never part of the report contract.\n- `--help` is an ordinary unknown option; no help mode exists.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-UnreadableLog.md ===\n# FEATURE: Unreadable Log\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261026 V1 |\n| Description | A log that cannot be opened or read exits 1 with nothing on standard output. |\n| Depends On  | FEATURE-LogReading.md, FEATURE-CommandLine.md |\n| Provides    | exit code 1 |\n| Consumes    | logread.Read, ddkstat <report> [--target] [--since] [--until] <log> |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nAn operational failure reading the log is reported differently from a usage error so that callers\ncan tell a bad invocation from a bad environment.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- A log path that does not exist, cannot be opened, or cannot be read exits 1.\n- A directory named as the log path is unreadable and exits 1 (decision Q-003, `exit-1`).\n- One diagnostic line is written to standard error; standard output stays empty.\n- Report output is buffered until the whole log has been read, so a read error after some lines\n  were consumed cannot leave partial standard output.\n- This applies to every report name.\n- The log is only ever opened for reading.\n\n## Required Go Tests\n\nThe build writes this test in package `./internal/cli`, using a reader that fails partway through:\n\n- `TestReadErrorLeavesStdoutEmpty`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC unreadable-log-missing-file ===\nIntent: A missing log exits 1 with empty standard output for every report.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nimport os\nimport tempfile\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\nmissing = os.path.join(tempfile.gettempdir(), \"ddkstat-no-such-log-unreadable.jsonl\")\nassert not os.path.exists(missing)\n\nfor report in (\"summary\", \"commands\", \"targets\", \"models\", \"daily\", \"failures\"):\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(report, missing)\n    print(report, code)\n    print(err)\n    assert code == 1, (report, code, out, err)\n    assert out == \"\", (report, out)\n=== END AC unreadable-log-missing-file ===\n\n=== AC unreadable-log-directory ===\nIntent: A directory named as the log exits 1 with empty standard output.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nimport os\nimport tempfile\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\ndirectory = tempfile.mkdtemp()\ntry:\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(\"summary\", directory)\n    print(code)\n    print(err)\n    assert code == 1, (code, out, err)\n    assert out == \"\", out\nfinally:\n    os.rmdir(directory)\n=== END AC unreadable-log-directory ===\n\n=== AC unreadable-log-no-partial-output ===\nIntent: A read error partway through the log leaves standard output empty.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/cli\", \"TestReadErrorLeavesStdoutEmpty\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC unreadable-log-no-partial-output ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- The log is never opened for writing and is never modified.\n- An unreadable log never writes to standard output.\n- An unreadable log exits 1, never 0 or 2.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===","ttft_ms":35623,"type":"result","duration_ms":71910,"uuid":"a91af7b4-0f0f-4e4b-a0e4-fc675822a744","ttft_stream_ms":5102,"time_to_request_ms":106,"first_content_frame_ms":5102,"queued_turn_count":0,"result_index":0,"model_usage_since_last_result":{"claude-sonnet-5-5":{"inputTokens":2,"outputTokens":10982,"cacheReadInputTokens":1568,"cacheCreationInputTokens":82800,"webSearchRequests":0,"costUSD":0.4411808,"contextWindow":1000000,"maxOutputTokens":128000,"thinkingTokens":3864,"canonicalModel":"claude-sonnet-5-5","provider":"firstParty","costBasis":"list"}}}
